- **prior active-phase:** `phase 52 (load-balancer-locality-weighted) IMPL done` (2026-07-06) — the SIXTH Load-balancing-family row BUILT end-to-end (subagent-driven in a worktree, lifecycle-state 3 → done); **ANCHORS ADR-0269** (§Decision/§Consequences landed IN-PLACE per ADR-0044). A new `internal/cluster/locality.go` `localityWeightedLB` wrapper `loadBalancer` (the phase-38.1 `subsetLB` structural precedent, generalized from a route-resolved metadata match to a build-time-fixed locality identity) partitions a cluster's endpoints by `LocalityLbEndpoints.locality` identity and weighted-random-draws a locality per `Pick`, scaled by that locality's live healthy-host fraction via the confirmed `effective_weight = configured_weight × min(1, (overprovisioning_factor/100) × healthy_fraction)` formula (AMEND-LW3) — delegating to a per-locality child `loadBalancer` built by the EXISTING `buildLeafLB` factory + a `flat` all-endpoints fallback child for the panic-mode (AMEND-LW4) and zero-total-weight (AMEND-LW2) paths. UNLIKE `subsetLB`, needs **ZERO new `Pick` parameters** (D-LW7 CONFIRMED byte-for-byte). `Endpoint` grows `Locality LocalityID`/`LocalityWeight uint32` (the SECOND per-endpoint dimension after phase-38's `Metadata`), captured by an extended `extractEndpoints`; `manager.go`'s `buildCluster` gains a SECOND wrap-after-switch site (`sc` hoisted once above both), gated by TWO new envoy-go-strict rejects (`zone_aware_lb_config` unsupported; `lb_subset_config` combined with `locality_weighted_lb_config` rejected — both CONFIRMED live departures at SPEC). Two design upgrades surfaced during IMPL, both landed and documented: **D-LW-HEALTHALLOC** (allocating `clusterHealth` unconditionally for a locality-weighted cluster widens the CONDITION under which `registerClusterMetrics`'s pre-existing unconditional health-registration block fires — `membership_healthy`/`lb_healthy_panic` now also register for such a cluster with zero `health_checks`; a widened condition on two pre-existing stat NAMES, not a stat-surface COUNT change) and **D-LW-OPF0** (`newLocalityWeightedLB` threads `(opf uint32, hasOPF bool)` rather than a bare `uint32`, since the `*wrapperspb.UInt32Value` pointer is already in scope before `.GetValue()` — an explicit `overprovisioning_factor: 0` is honored LITERALLY, degrading the whole mechanism to flat fallback unconditionally, rather than silently defaulting to 140; an upgrade beyond the SPEC's own leaning-to-accept-the-departure default). A previously-undocumented structural finding (the child-local-panic coverage boundary — a locality's own leaf child evaluates its OWN `clusterHealth.inPanic` over its OWN endpoint sub-slice, independent of the wrapper's cluster-wide panic gate) is documented and unit-tested, not fixed (fixing it would require passing a per-locality "panic disabled" view into `buildLeafLB`'s children, contradicting the ZERO-new-`Pick`-parameter design). The new `test/fixtures/0095-lb-locality-weighted` cross-side differential (2 localities × 5 hosts, 2:1 weight) proves BOTH arms live: (a) an all-healthy static weight-ratio band and (b) a health-degradation shift band (reusing the phase-39 poll-to-convergence-and-warmup pattern), each 20/20 flake-free and `-race` clean — via TWO controller-reperformed deliberate breaks (skip-the-wrap; freeze-effective-weight-at-100%-healthy) against the frozen worktree HEAD, each failing exactly as predicted, then cleanly restored. Building this fixture surfaced and root-caused THREE genuine bugs (not papered over): an unset `no_traffic_healthy_interval` stalling arm(b)'s convergence poll (defaults to `no_traffic_interval`, 60s, not `interval`); a health-blind warmup gate (`toggleResponder` always answers 200 on the data path, so the original K-consecutive-200s check was a no-op — fixed by making it host-identity-aware); and a `-count=N` singleton-state leak across repeated fixture invocations in the same process (the memoized region-A `toggleResponder`s never reset to healthy — fixed by resetting inside `ensureRegionA()` on every call, before containers boot). A genuine PLAN gap (the `test/differential/runner_test.go` blank-import registration for fixture `0095`, never mentioned in any of the PLAN's 10 tasks — without it the fixture would have been silently skipped forever) was found and fixed by the controller before dispatching that task. The §10 D-LW1..D-LW8 empirical pins EXECUTED IN-SESSION via TWO PARALLEL probe agents live against `envoyproxy/envoy:contrib-v1.37.2` on a Docker bridge network (`reference_docker_probe_bridge_network`). **AMEND-LW2 (REFUTATION + CORRECTION of the BRAINSTORM's assumption):** an omitted `load_balancing_weight` does NOT default to `1` — the reference assigns that locality ZERO load whenever a sibling locality has an explicit weight (confirmed both by the go-control-plane proto doc comment, "the locality is assigned no load," AND live traffic: A=2-explicit/B-omitted → A got all 900 requests, B got 0); when NO locality anywhere sets a weight, the mechanism degrades to flat round-robin (both-omitted → an even ~1:1 split). envoy-go unifies both outcomes with ONE rule: a locality's raw captured weight (0 if unset, zero substitution logic) naturally yields an empty weighted-draw bucket, and a zero TOTAL effective weight across every locality falls back to the same flat all-endpoints child panic mode uses. **AMEND-LW3 (CONFIRMATION of the hypothesized formula):** `effective_weight = configured_weight × min(1, (overprovisioning_factor/100) × healthy_fraction)` matched a live 6-point degradation curve (100→0% healthy) to within <1 percentage point at every measurement; an OPF-140-vs-100 A/B at an identical 60%-healthy state gave clearly different results (62.8% vs 54.0%), PROVING `ClusterLoadAssignment.Policy.overprovisioning_factor` (default 140) must be genuinely consumed, not hardcoded. **AMEND-LW4 (CONFIRMATION):** cluster-wide panic (the EXISTING, UNMODIFIED `clusterHealth.inPanic` gate) fully bypasses BOTH health-filtering and locality-weighting (both localities degraded to 1/5 healthy → traffic spread near-evenly ~49.4%/50.6% across all 10 hosts, not the configured 2:1). **AMEND-LW5 (REFUTATION DOWN):** the anticipated "small 0-2" stat-surface delta is a confirmed **ZERO** — a live `/stats` scrape found no dedicated locality-weighted stat group in the reference; stat surface STAYS **1200** exactly, the family's THIRD zero-stat-delta phase. **AMEND-LW1 (CONFIRMATION):** both planned rejects are genuine envoy-go-strict departures — the reference ACCEPTS `lb_subset_config` + `locality_weighted_lb_config` together but SILENTLY overrides locality weighting once subset engages (a live 3:1-weighted test gave an exact 1:1 split); `zone_aware_lb_config` alone is a fully legitimate, separately-working reference feature. Architecture (D-LW7 CONFIRMED): `internal/cluster/locality.go`'s `localityWeightedLB` — a wrapper (the phase-38 `subsetLB` structural precedent) needing **ZERO new `Pick` parameters**, forwarding `(hashKey, hasHash, match, hasMatch)` straight through to a per-locality child exactly as `subsetLB` already does; `Endpoint` grows `Locality`/`LocalityWeight` (the `Metadata` precedent); a second wrap-after-switch site in `manager.go`'s `buildCluster`. Differential: the NEW `0095-lb-locality-weighted` fixture (2 localities × 5 hosts, 2:1 weight) — a per-side static-ratio band arm + a health-degradation shift arm (reusing the phase-39 poll-to-convergence-and-warmup pattern) proving the CONFIRMED formula's predicted shift. 1 ADR anticipated (**ADR-0269**, single-ADR reuse shape, confirmed correct; §Context drafted at SPEC §13). `general-purpose` spec-document-reviewer pass: every file:line citation + proto field number + baseline count VERIFIED EXACT against HEAD — APPROVED-WITH-FOLDS after one non-blocking wording fold (the `newPCGRNG`/`router_weighted.go` "reused by" phrasing corrected to "duplicated independently," matching that file's own doc comment). byte-stable when unconfigured; the +0 no-new-stat guard) + the driver-owned `test/helpers/metricsservice` h2c receiver + the `0089-stats-sink-metrics-service` cross-side EXACT differential. **As-built corrections (controller-found during Task-9/10 verification, root-caused per `superpowers:systematic-debugging`):** (1) the `0089` differential uses **TWO driver-owned per-side `MetricsService` receivers** (one port per side), NOT the single shared receiver of the event-driven OTLP/Zipkin trace fixtures — the metrics_service sink flushes PERIODICALLY, so the reference proxy streams for the whole test and a shared receiver would contaminate the subject snapshot AND silently defeat subject-side deliberate breaks (a broken subject would still read the reference's 7s); The BRAINSTORM predecessor — `phase 43.2b (h2-connection-pool) IMPL done` (squash `676e17cf`; the Upstream-robustness family CLOSED). ALSO superseded — the prior IMPL-done note:

- **prior active-phase:** `phase 43.2b (h2-connection-pool) IMPL done` (2026-06-24) — the 10-task TDD PLAN executed end-to-end subagent-driven (fresh implementer per task), the six-gate GREEN, the full **82-dir differential GREEN** (lifecycle-state 3 → 4; **row 43 (`connection-pooling`) FLIPS `in-progress → done`** — ALL legs 43.1 + 43.2a + 43.2b landed ⇒ the **Upstream-robustness family (phases 39–43) CLOSES**). As built: **ADR-0254** — graceful GOAWAY-driven HTTP/2 upstream connection rotation (the H2-multiplex-pool drain lifecycle ADR-0253's minimal liveness DEFERRED). The codec exposes three read-only accessors over the existing `goawayCh`/`ctx` — `GoneAway() bool` + `GoneAwayCh() <-chan struct{}` + `Done() <-chan struct{}` (NO `cc.cancel()`, so an in-flight stream below `LastStreamID` still completes; the `Done()` accessor REQUIRED because `cc.ctx` is unexported from package `cluster`). A GOAWAY'd conn becomes DRAINING: (1) **admission-skip** — the two ESTABLISHED-conn sites (`findStreamHitLocked` + `h2PromoteLocked`'s stream-slot branch) add `&& !pc.cc.GoneAway()`; (2) **in-flight drain-close-on-last-stream** — `makeRelease`'s eager-close generalizes `pc.cc.Closed() && inFlight==0` → `(Closed() || GoneAway()) && inFlight==0`; (3) **idle prompt-close** — a per-conn drain-watcher goroutine (`watchDrain`, spawned in `dialAndPool` after the pool append) `select`s `GoneAwayCh()` vs `Done()` and evicts an IDLE draining conn promptly under `h2PoolMu` (re-checking `findPooledLocked != nil` — the double-evict guard); (4) **lazy replacement** via the established MISS path (`upstream_cx_http2_total` ++; permit re-homed). The rotation is observed ENTIRELY via `upstream_cx_http2_total` + the base `upstream_cx_*` (NO new GOAWAY-named stat — AMEND-H2B-1; the reference has no `goaway_received` + reads `http2.goaway_sent==0` on a graceful drain-close). The FIRST codec→cluster stat wiring: `cluster.<name>.http2.rx_reset` + `http2.tx_reset` (useH2-gated, **1185 → 1187**) injected via the variadic `h2.WithResetHooks(onRx, onTx func())` option applied PRE-`readLoop` (D-H2B-WIRING — a setter would race the running readLoop; the codec stays stats-agnostic, no `internal/stats` import) at `dialPooledH2To`; the kept `DialH2` passes nil. The codec increments at its existing RST sites (`dispatchFrame` `RSTStreamFrame` rx + `RoundTrip` `ctx.Done` CANCEL tx). NEW BackendKind **38** (`H2GoawayResponder` — a raw-`golang.org/x/net/http2`-framer h2c backend with `/__release` + `/__goaway`[NO_ERROR] + `/__rst`[INTERNAL_ERROR] gates; the kind-37 `H2HoldResponder` uses `h2c.NewHandler`/`http.Server` which CANNOT emit on-demand GOAWAY/RST frames). Differential **`0080-h2-goaway-rotation`** (cross-side EXACT — a six-stage sleepless drive: establish ⇒ in-flight drain ⇒ idle drain ⇒ rx_reset prong ⇒ tx_reset prong ⇒ quiesce; cross-side EXACT `upstream_cx_http2_total==2` + `http2.rx_reset==1` + `http2.tx_reset==1`). **Two documented FAITHFUL fixture-drive departures (recorded in ADR-0254 — NOT behavior divergences):** (A) `/__release` broadened to a backend-global broadcast; (B) `tx_reset` driven via a `per_try_timeout` (envoy-go dispatches H2 downstream streams on the connection-level ctx, so a downstream cancel doesn't propagate to the upstream `RoundTrip` ctx — the 42.2b "honors ctx.Deadline not ctx.Done on the upstream" precedent; the `per_try_timeout` reaches the SAME codec CANCEL site, cross-side EXACT). **The per-conn drain-watcher's idle-close is a recorded COVERAGE BOUNDARY:** 3 of 4 `-count=1` deliberate breaks bit (admission-skip → `cx_http2_total`, rx_reset, tx_reset); the watcher idle-close break does NOT bite because `makeRelease`'s eager-close masks it (the `/__goaway` control request rides the target conn as an in-flight stream, so its release eager-closes the conn before the watcher acts). The watcher remains CORRECT + ESSENTIAL in production for the truly-idle-GOAWAY case and is RIGOROUSLY UNIT-proven (a 1000-iteration contested race test + an idle-prompt-close test); a watcher-isolating differential is a recorded hardening item. 20/20 flake-free + `-race`-clean. ADR-0254 §Decision/§Consequences landed IN-PLACE per ADR-0044. As-built counts: stat **1185 → 1187** (+2 — `http2.rx_reset` + `http2.tx_reset`, useH2-gated; non-H2 stays **1183**) / fixtures **81 → 82** (`0080-h2-goaway-rotation`) / fuzzers **42 documented → 43** (RECONCILED to the actual `^func Fuzz` count per `reference_fuzzer_count_docs_drift` — 43.2b adds none; D-H2B-FUZZER-RECONCILE) / BackendKind tail **37 → 38** (`H2GoawayResponder`) / DECISIONS tail **ADR-0253 → ADR-0254** (next-free **ADR-0255**); ZERO new packages + ZERO new go.mod modules. The full six-gate GREEN (build/vet/gofmt/lint clean; unit+integration GREEN; full 82-dir differential GREEN). **DEFERRED hardening items (recorded in ADR-0254):** `http2.goaway_sent` (would diverge — AMEND-H2B-3), `http2.stream_refused_errors` (reference-dormant), `min(local,peer)` peer-min enforcement, `nextStreamID` 2^31 retirement, + a watcher-isolating differential. **NEXT → a FRESH BRAINSTORM for a NEW subject** (the Upstream-robustness family is CLOSED — 0 candidates remain; the next phase opens a NEW family per the family-closure precedent; `superpowers:brainstorming`, a fresh worktree off master per `feedback_git_worktrees`). an H1 downstream → h2c upstream cluster `c_h2` with `max_concurrent_streams: 100`; a raw-`golang.org/x/net/http2`-framer h2c backend with `/goaway` + `/rst_last` control gates; sequenced single-shot + `/stats`-delta scrapes; ran in a throwaway worktree off master per `feedback_git_worktrees`, discarded after pinning). **THE HEADLINE EMPIRICAL REVERSAL (AMEND-H2B-1..4) — the probe REVERSED the brainstorm's stat scope DOWN:** (AMEND-H2B-1) a peer GOAWAY is observed ENTIRELY via the connection-lifecycle counters — `upstream_cx_close_notify` ++ IMMEDIATELY on receipt (drain mark), then for an IDLE conn a PROMPT local close (`upstream_cx_destroy`/`_destroy_local` ++, `cx_active` --) and for an IN-FLIGHT conn the conn HELD OPEN until the last in-flight stream completes then `upstream_cx_destroy_local` ++ (`_with_active_rq` STAYS 0 — graceful), replacement LAZY on the next request (`upstream_cx_http2_total` 1→2) — with **NO `http2.*` counter moving during a clean rotation** and **NO `goaway_received` counter existing** in the reference's 23-name `http2.*` subtree; (AMEND-H2B-2) of the brainstorm's FOUR candidate reset/goaway counters only **TWO** are LIVE + cross-side assertable — `http2.tx_reset` (downstream-cancel → the codec's existing `RoundTrip` ctx.Done `WriteRSTStream(id, CANCEL)`) + `http2.rx_reset` (backend RST → the codec's existing `dispatchFrame` `RSTStreamFrame` case → 503, conn survives) ⇒ register exactly those two, surface **1185 → 1187 (+2, REVISED DOWN from the brainstorm's ~1190/~5)**; (AMEND-H2B-3) `http2.goaway_sent` DEFERRED (the reference reads 0 through a full drain-close — envoy-go's `Close()`-emitted GOAWAY would DIVERGE non-zero) + `http2.stream_refused_errors` DEFERRED (reference-DORMANT: it proactively respects peer `SETTINGS_MAX_CONCURRENT_STREAMS` + opens a conn-per-concurrent-cold-request prefetch, so it never RECEIVES a REFUSED_STREAM ⇒ a cross-side EXACT assert would be vacuous-at-0); (AMEND-H2B-4) the `0080` differential is cross-side EXACT under the sequenced-barrier drive — NO subject-EXACT + cross-ROBUST fallback needed (`cx_http2_total==2` post-rotation, `rx_reset==1`/`tx_reset==1` on their dedicated prongs). **The design (ADR-0254):** the codec exposes `GoneAway() bool` + `GoneAwayCh() <-chan struct{}` (read-only over the existing `goawayCh` — NO `cc.cancel()`, so an in-flight stream below `LastStreamID` still completes); the two ESTABLISHED-conn admission sites add `&& !pc.cc.GoneAway()`; `makeRelease`'s eager-close generalizes `pc.cc.Closed() && inFlight==0` → `(Closed() || GoneAway()) && inFlight==0` (the in-flight drain-close-on-last-stream); a per-conn drain-watcher goroutine spawned in `dialAndPool` after the `h2Pool[addr]` append (`select { <-cc.GoneAwayCh() [evict-if-idle under `h2PoolMu`, re-checking `findPooledLocked != nil`] ; <-cc.ctx.Done() [exit, evicted-by-another-path] }`) closes the IDLE draining conn promptly; lazy replacement via the established MISS path; the FIRST codec→cluster stat wiring injects the `http2.rx_reset`/`http2.tx_reset` handles at the `dialPooledH2To`→`NewClientConn` dial seam (codec increments at its existing RST sites). **PLAN D-questions:** D-H2B-{BACKEND [extend `H2HoldResponder` kind 37 with `/__goaway`+`/__rst` gates vs new kind 38], WATCHER-LIFECYCLE, WIRING [`NewClientConn` constructor-param vs setter; `DialH2` passes nil — nil-guarded increments], SPLIT-FINAL [43.2b anticipated flat ~8–10 tasks / ~150–220 LoC], FUZZER-RECONCILE [documented-42-vs-actual-43 — reconcile at this family-closing leg or carry forward], BYTESTAB [`0004`/`0079` stay GREEN — `0079` does not trigger GOAWAY], CXSTATS [which `upstream_cx_*` close/destroy counters envoy-go emits — grep shows it does NOT emit `cx_close_notify`/`cx_destroy_local`, so the `0080` EXACT prong asserts `cx_http2_total` + the two reset counters which BOTH sides emit]}. **ALL COUNTS UNCHANGED at this SPEC commit (docs-only):** stat surface **1185** / fixtures **81** / fuzzers **42** (documented; actual `^func Fuzz` 43) / BackendKind tail **37** / DECISIONS tail **ADR-0253** (next-free **ADR-0254**). - **prior active-phase:** `phase 43.2b (h2-connection-pool) BRAINSTORM done` (2026-06-24) — the SECOND/FINAL sub-leg of the H2-connection-pool leg brainstormed (lifecycle-state 0 → 1; row 43 STAYS `in-progress` — leg 43.1 done, 43.2a done, **43.2b brainstormed**). `docs/envoy-go/phases/43.2-h2-connection-pool/BRAINSTORM-43.2b.md` authored in the house BRAINSTORM shape + spec-document-reviewer APPROVED (first-iteration; ALL load-bearing code claims verified true against `h2pool.go`/`client.go` — the GOAWAY'd-but-alive conn is invisible to the 43.2a admission scan because `dispatchFrame`'s `GoAwayFrame` case closes `goawayCh` + finishes streams above `LastStreamID` but does NOT `cc.cancel()`, so `Closed()` stays false; 2 advisory recs, 1 FOLDED into D-H2B-WATCHER-LIFECYCLE [`cc.ctx.Done()` is the natural "evicted-by-another-path" watcher signal + re-check `findPooledLocked != nil` before evicting to dodge the double-evict race with `EvictH2ConnOnError`/`makeRelease`]). **The design forks settled via live dialogue:** (Q-scope → CORE-ONLY) GOAWAY rotation + the behavior-driven `http2.*` reset/goaway stats ONLY; `min(local,peer)` enforcement (AMEND-H2-5) + `nextStreamID` 2^31-exhaustion retirement (M-12/Task-11) DEFERRED as recorded hardening items (keeps 43.2b a single flat leg under the ADR-0045 gate — no further sub-split). (Q-drain-detection → WATCHER) a per-conn drain-watcher goroutine spawned at pool insertion in `dialAndPool` (`select { <-cc.GoneAwayCh() ; <-cc.ctx.Done() }`) that on GOAWAY marks the conn draining + evicts-if-idle — chosen over poll-on-admission (an IDLE GOAWAY'd conn has no `releaseStream` to ever fire + ctx never cancels ⇒ a slow conn leak) + over a codec `onGoAway` callback (inverts pool→codec layering). (Q-stats-scope → BEHAVIOR-SUBSET) register only the `http2.*` counters the codec drives live: candidates `http2.goaway_sent`/`rx_reset`/`tx_reset`/`stream_refused_errors` (every one LIVE + assertable) — chosen over the full ~24-name reference subtree (≈20 would register dead-at-0 — the codec lacks the flood/messaging-error paths) + over goaway_sent-only. (Q-stat-wiring → INJECT-HANDLES) the pool injects the cluster's `http2.*` stat handles into the `ClientConn` at dial (the FIRST codec→cluster stat wiring — 43.2a drove ALL stats from the pool layer) — chosen over codec-side atomic accumulators the pool reads-and-deltas (lossy for live reset counters). (Q-differential-strategy → SEQUENCED-EXACT-FIRST) drive the rotation as discrete poll-to-converge phases behind release barriers targeting cross-side EXACT (fire streams → barrier → trigger GOAWAY → poll the drained conn out of admission + the `http2.*` deltas → fire a fresh request → poll the replacement conn in → release → drain), with a subject-EXACT + cross-ROBUST fallback per-count if the SPEC probe shows a residual GOAWAY-vs-next-request race — chosen over subject-EXACT-upfront + over behavioral-only. **The `0080-h2-goaway-rotation` differential** MUST use an H2 (TLS+ALPN-h2) DOWNSTREAM listener (the 0004 PKI shape — the H2 pool engages ONLY on an H2 downstream, `reference_h2_pool_downstream_codec_gate`); the GOAWAY-capable backend (extend `H2HoldResponder` BackendKind 37 with a `/__goaway` gate vs a new BackendKind 38) is a PLAN D-question (D-H2B-BACKEND — the D-H2-BACKEND precedent). **SPEC live-probe pins (D-H2B-{STATS,ROTATION,EXACT,GOAWAYSENT})** to execute against `contrib-v1.37.2` per `reference_docker_probe_bridge_network` (the exact `http2.*` names + the `+N`; the reference's lazy-replacement `upstream_cx_*`/`cx_destroy` sequence; per-count cross-side determinism; whether `goaway_sent` is driven by OUR `Close()`-emitted GOAWAY [a peer-GOAWAY'd conn has `goawaySent==false` ⇒ Close still emits]). **PLAN D-questions:** D-H2B-{BACKEND, WATCHER-LIFECYCLE, WIRING [constructor-param vs setter; does `DialH2` pass nil], SPLIT-FINAL, FUZZER-RECONCILE [the documented-42-vs-actual-43 `^func Fuzz` drift — reconcile at this family-closing leg or carry forward], BYTESTAB [`0004`/`0079` stay GREEN — `0079` does not trigger GOAWAY]}. **ALL COUNTS UNCHANGED at this BRAINSTORM commit (docs-only):** stat surface **1185** / fixtures **81** / fuzzers **42** (documented; actual `^func Fuzz` 43) / BackendKind tail **37** / DECISIONS tail **ADR-0253** (next-free **ADR-0254**). The BRAINSTORM predecessor — `phase 43.2a (h2-connection-pool) IMPL done` (commit `30b3aad1`). ALSO superseded — the prior IMPL-done note:

- **prior active-phase:** `phase 43.2a (h2-connection-pool) IMPL done` (2026-06-23) — the 12-task TDD PLAN executed end-to-end subagent-driven (fresh implementer per task + two-stage review), the six-gate GREEN, the full **81-dir differential GREEN** (lifecycle-state 3 → 4; row 43 STAYS `in-progress` — leg 43.1 done, **43.2a done**, 43.2b pending). As built: **ADR-0253** — a per-endpoint HTTP/2 multiplex `ClientConn` pool over the 43.1 permit substrate (ADR-0252), **SUPERSEDING ADR-0056** (per-request-fresh H2 dial). `Cluster.h2Pool map[string][]*pooledH2Conn` (keyed by `ep.Addr()`, guarded by `h2PoolMu`) holds multiplexed `*h2.ClientConn`s; a new conn opens only when every existing conn is saturated at the cluster's OWN `http2_protocol_options.max_concurrent_streams` (the LOCAL cap C — AMEND-H2-1, the headline live finding: the reference keys multi-conn growth off the local cap, NOT peer SETTINGS ⇒ `ceil(K/C)` deterministic, cross-side EXACT-able), default `1<<30` (AMEND-H2-3, single-conn multiplex). A SEPARATE stream-aware pending queue (woken on stream-free via `h2PromoteLocked` OR conn-close; bounded by `max_pending_requests`; queue-full → `errConnPoolOverflow` → 503 + `upstream_rq_pending_overflow`); `tryAcquireConnSlot` (NON-blocking) keeps H2 OUT of the 43.1 `connPool.waiters`. **Two key as-built mechanisms the SPEC under-specified (mid-IMPL fixes):** (Task 9.5) **connect-time dial coalescing** — a "connecting" tier (`Cluster.h2Connecting`, `connectingH2Conn{ready, cc, err, promised}`) so K CONCURRENT acquires ATTACH to establishing conns rather than each dialing ⇒ exactly `ceil(K/C)` conns under burst (matching the reference; without it the pool over-dialed to K); (Task 7.5) **a single ctx-aware LB pick** threaded through the dial seam (ONE `c.lb.Pick(hashKeyFrom(ctx),…,subsetMatchFrom(ctx),…)` firing exactly once per acquire, fixing an earlier ctx-blind double-pick defect) ⇒ conns keyed+attributed to the dialed endpoint + hash/subset/source_ip affinity honored. The router rewire moved BOTH `router_h2.go` H2 sites off `DialH2`+`defer cc.Close()` onto `AcquireH2Stream`+`release()`+evict-on-error; the legacy `doH2` was proven DEAD + REMOVED (completing the ADR-0056 supersession IN CODE). Minimal liveness only (`Closed()`-skip + evict-on-RoundTrip-error); GOAWAY rotation + the `http2.*` reset/goaway stats + `min(local,peer)` peer-min are DEFERRED to 43.2b (ADR-0254). **A SPEC §8.1 topology correction:** envoy-go selects the router action by the DOWNSTREAM listener codec — the H2 pool engages ONLY on an H2 (TLS+ALPN-h2) downstream listener (there is NO H1-down→H2-up bridge); `0079` uses an H2 downstream accordingly. **2 new stats** (useH2-gated): `upstream_cx_http2_total` (counter) + `http2.streams_active` (gauge); NO `upstream_cx_http2_active` (AMEND-H2-2) ⇒ an H2 cluster emits **1185**, a non-H2 cluster stays at **1183** (confirmed by `TestRegisterClusterMetrics_H2Stats`). Differential **`0079-h2-multiplex-pool`** (cross-side EXACT: `ceil(K/C)` conns + `http2.streams_active==K` both sides; an overflow prong → downstream 503 + `upstream_rq_pending_overflow` delta) + NEW BackendKind **37** (`H2HoldResponder` — h2c hold-and-release, `SETTINGS_MAX_CONCURRENT_STREAMS: 1000`); 3 `-count=1` deliberate breaks (all bit) + 20/20 flake-free + `-race`-clean. ADR-0253 §Decision/§Consequences landed IN-PLACE per ADR-0044; ADR-0056 status header edited to `SUPERSEDED by ADR-0253`. As built: **ADR-0252** — `max_connections` HARD-CAP + `max_pending_requests` bounded wait-queue, the project's FIRST standing upstream request queue. A per-cluster DEFAULT-priority `connPool` (`internal/cluster/connpool.go` — a SIBLING to the phase-41 `circuitBreaker`; `pool *connPool` field on `circuitBreaker`) with a `sync.Mutex`-guarded `activeConns` counter + a FIFO of per-waiter BUFFERED cap-1 wake channels. A connection-creation permit is try-acquired at the two creation boundaries (`Cluster.Dial` raw-dial + the `AcquireH1` pool-MISS); a pool HIT reuses an already-counted conn (no permit). At the cap the request PENDS (bounded by `max_pending_requests`), woken via DIRECT permit-handoff on `connWithGauge.Close` (`connDec` → `pool.releaseConn` → pop head + send) or on `PutIdleH1` idle-return (CLOSE-and-wake when a waiter is queued, routing BOTH wake sources through the single permit mechanism); ctx-cancel re-locks + `removeWaiterLocked`, and if already dequeued drains + gives the permit back (raced-permit-never-leaks). Queue-full → `cluster.IsConnPoolOverflow` sentinel → fail-fast 503 + `upstream_rq_pending_overflow` (the shared phase-41 counter). **The load-bearing DEPARTURE (AMEND-CP1 — `reference_max_connections_soft_breaker`):** the reference's `max_connections` is a SOFT breaker (`cx_active` can exceed the cap with timing slop); envoy-go implements a CLEAN HARD-CAP as a documented departure recorded in ADR-0252. Byte-identical when no `circuit_breakers` (the full 79→80-dir differential is the regression anchor). **The 2 new stat shapes:** `upstream_rq_pending_active` gauge + `upstream_rq_pending_total` counter (+2 ⇒ **1181 → 1183**); 3 existing shapes (`cx_open`, `rq_pending_open`, `upstream_cx_overflow`) ACTIVATED from emit-0 (+0 surface delta — already registered). Each in-flight attempt is a goroutine (body replay + `do{H1,H2}ClusterAction` + `matches` classify + the CB-admission/`RecordUpstreamResult`/`retry_budget` seams) with a per-attempt HEDGE-TRIGGER timer (`time.AfterFunc` at `per_try_timeout`) that on expiry launches a budget-counted hedge LEAVING the original in flight; a BUFFERED first-acceptable-wins collector returns the first non-retriable result + cancels/drains losers (no leak, `-race`-clean). The `hedge_policy` parsed in `parseHedgePolicy` (`hcm/config.go`) with the vhost fallback + carried on `clusterRouteAction.hedgePolicy` (`hcm/actions.go`); the `hp *HedgePolicy` threaded through the `H{1,2}ClusterAction` signatures. **The load-bearing invariants held as-built:** (AMEND-H1 — #1) a hedged per-try-timeout increments `upstream_rq_retry` (+ `_retry_backoff_exponential`, NO `time.Sleep`), NEVER `upstream_rq_per_try_timeout` (which STAYS **0** under hedge mode — the per-try-timeout is a hedge-LAUNCH not an abandonment; the hedge path uses a HEDGE-TRIGGER timer NOT the 42.2a cancelling-deadline-+-discriminator); (AMEND-H2) `upstream_rq_retry_limit_exceeded` under hedging = "no more hedges can launch" NOT a final failure — the in-flight original can still WIN with a 200 (hedging RECOVERS a slow request where 42.2a sequential ABANDONS it to a 504); (D-S422B-2 — a recorded departure) the H1/H2 loser-cancel ASYMMETRY [`doH1ClusterAction` honors only `ctx.Deadline()` ⇒ an H1 loser completes on backend-response (the `0077` `/__release` guarantees termination, NO active H1-loser socket reset); `-race`-clean); (D-S422B-3/AMEND-H4) the `initial_requests`/`additional_request_chance` fan-out is reference-no-op'd ⇒ SUBJECT-SIDE (UNIT-tested — NO `0078` dir), each fan-out attempt `TryAcquireRetry`-gated BEFORE the goroutine spawn (acquire-gates-spawn caps the live goroutine count under the `retry_budget`); `initial_requests:0` ⇒ a `gte:1` reject, `:3` accepted) ⇒ the fan-out is SUBJECT-SIDE; (H5) NO new stat — surface STAYS **1181** (no `*hedge*` counter; `retry_or_shadow_abandoned`/`upstream_rq_timeout`/`upstream_rq_per_try_idle_timeout` all 0); each in-flight attempt is a goroutine (body replay + `do{H1,H2}ClusterAction` + `matches` classify + CB-admission/outcome seams) with a per-attempt HEDGE-TRIGGER timer that on `per_try_timeout` launches a budget-counted hedge (`upstream_rq_retry`++) leaving the original in flight; a first-acceptable-wins collector returns the first non-retriable result + cancels/drains losers (no leak, `-race`-clean); the BUDGET-COUNTED fan-out (subject-side; the `retry_budget` caps the goroutine fan-out under acquire-gates-spawn). - **prior active-phase:** `phase 42.2a (per_try_timeout) IMPL done` (2026-06-21) — the 11-task TDD PLAN executed end-to-end subagent-driven (fresh implementer per task + two-stage review), the six-gate GREEN, the full **78-dir differential GREEN** (lifecycle-state 3 → 4; row 42 STAYS `in-progress`). As built: **ADR-0250** — `RetryPolicy.per_try_timeout` (field 3), a per-attempt deadline over the 42.1 retry loop; the project's FIRST request-scoped deadline (envoy-go does NOT implement the global `RouteAction.timeout`). The 42.1 `retryExecutorH1`/`retryExecutorH2` (`internal/filter/http/router/retry.go`) wrap each `do{H1,H2}ClusterAction` call in a child `context.WithTimeout(ctx, rp.perTryTimeout)` (when `>0`); on the child deadline expiring while the PARENT ctx is alive, the attempt is a per-try-timeout — a synthesized **504** (overriding the H1 driver's 502 / the H2 `Status:0` CANCEL sentinel), `IncUpstreamRqPerTryTimeout()`, retried via `perTryTimeoutRetriable()` (`matches(504,false) || retryReset`) under {5xx, gateway-error, retriable-status-codes∋504, **reset**} — NOT `connect-failure`-alone, so `parseRetryOn` SPLITS the 42.1-fused `connect-failure`/`reset` into `retryConnectFail` + a new `retryReset` (the 5th `retryOnBits` value; `matches` localOrigin arm `(retryConnectFail|retryReset)` preserves 42.1 dial-refusal-under-both). **The discriminator is RACE-ROBUST** (an IMPL-discovered refinement over the SPEC's simpler `errors.Is(attemptCtx.Err(), DeadlineExceeded)`, which was reproducibly racy under `-race`: the H1 driver propagates the child deadline onto the upstream socket, so the socket-I/O-timeout-broken read can return BEFORE context's timer stamps `Err()`): `timedOut := perTryTimeout>0 && ctx.Err()==nil && <failure-guard> && (errors.Is(attemptCtx.Err(), DeadlineExceeded) || !time.Now().Before(attemptDeadline))` where `<failure-guard>` is `resp.localOrigin` (H1) / `resp.localOrigin || resp.Status==0` (H2 — the timeout manifests as the `Status:0` sentinel, localOrigin=false) so a clean upstream reply is NEVER misclassified, the wall-clock fallback is monotonic-clock-immune to the timer lag, and it is read BEFORE the explicit `cancel()` (NOT defer — no timer-leak); the PARENT-alive guard preserves the 42.1 H2 `Status:0` client-cancel-not-retried invariant. The `per_try_timeout` is parsed in `parseRetryPolicy` (`hcm/config.go`, guarded `.AsDuration()`) onto a WIDENED `NewRetryPolicy` (6th param); `≤0`/unset ⇒ no bound (byte-stable), a NEGATIVE value ⇒ a route-scoped reject (`route: %q: retry_policy: per_try_timeout must not be negative`, the shared `ErrMsgPerTryTimeoutNegative` const). The **`upstream_rq_per_try_timeout` counter** is the 6th `retryStats` member via `EnsureRetryStats` (`cluster.go`) — registered on EVERY retry cluster (emit-0 where no `per_try_timeout`), ++ per timed-out attempt. The driver code is UNCHANGED (the H1 driver already `SetDeadline`s the socket `router.go:659-661`; the H2 driver already returns `Status:0` on a ctx deadline `router_h2.go:111`); NO `circuitbreaker.go` change. **Byte-identical when `per_try_timeout ≤ 0`** (the 77→78 differential is the regression anchor). DEPARTURE (D-S422A-8): the H1-records-a-local-origin-502-on-timeout vs H2-records-none asymmetry (the `0076` asserts neither `upstream_rq_502`/`upstream_rq_5xx`). Differential **`0076-per-try-timeout`** (cross-side: a SINGLE held `BlockingHoldResponder` host past a small `per_try_timeout` ⇒ deterministic exhaustion to a final 504; `upstream_rq_per_try_timeout==N+1` + `upstream_rq_retry==N` + `_retry_limit_exceeded==1` + `upstream_rq_total==N+1`, cross-side-EXACT [single host, offset-irrelevant]; `/__release` drains the parked attempts; NO new BackendKind); 2 `-count=1` deliberate breaks (A: per_try_timeout not threaded ⇒ the held read hangs to a transport timeout; B: `IncUpstreamRqPerTryTimeout` no-op ⇒ the cross-side delta assert fails) + 20/20 flake-free. ADR-0250 §Decision/§Consequences landed IN-PLACE per ADR-0044 (refined to reflect the as-built race-robust discriminator). As built: **ADR-0249** — HTTP retries (`RouteAction.retry_policy` / `VirtualHost` field-16 fallback) as a re-attempt loop WRAPPING the existing single-attempt driver: the project's FIRST request-replay control plane + its FIRST feature that RECOVERS a single request. A `retryExecutor` (`internal/filter/http/router/retry.go` — the `retry_on` tokenizer + `retryOnBits` bitset + the `matches(status, localOrigin)` classifier [`localOrigin` distinguishes a synthesized connect/reset from a 5xx RESPONSE] + the `NewRetryPolicy` ctor + the exponential full-jitter backoff [base 25ms / max 10×base; `max_interval < base_interval` reject]) wrapping `doH1ClusterAction` (`router.go`) / `doH2ClusterAction` (`router_h2.go`) + the weighted path (`router_weighted.go`): the `retry_policy` parsed in `buildRouterAction` (`hcm/config.go`) with the `VirtualHost` fallback threaded `buildRouteTable`→`buildAction`→`buildRouterAction`, carried on `clusterRouteAction` (`hcm/actions.go`), the `rp *RetryPolicy` threaded through the `H{1,2}ClusterAction` signatures; on a retriable outcome with `num_retries` (default 1) + `retry_budget` slots remaining it sleeps the backoff, replays the already-buffered request body (the ADR-0076 1 MiB buffer; the HCM 413s an over-cap body BEFORE the router, so the replay source is always ≤1MiB — NO over-cap guard needed), and re-invokes the driver (re-running CB admission + `RecordUpstreamResult` per attempt). The **+5 `upstream_rq_retry*` counters** (`upstream_rq_retry`/`_retry_success`/`_retry_limit_exceeded`/`_retry_backoff_exponential`/`_retry_backoff_ratelimited`) register SCOPED to retry-policy-bearing clusters via the lazy idempotent `Cluster.EnsureRetryStats()` (a departure — keeps non-retry clusters byte-stable; +5 per retry cluster ⇒ +10 for `0075`'s two). The **`retry_budget` activation** on the phase-41 `circuitBreaker`: a cluster-level `activeRetries atomic.Int64` + `tryAcquireRetry`/`releaseRetry` (`activeRetries < max(min_retry_concurrency, ⌈budget_percent% × activeRequests⌉)`; defaults 20%/3) flips the 2 phase-41 emit-0 stats LIVE (`circuit_breakers.<priority>.rq_retry_open` gauge + `upstream_rq_retry_overflow` counter), distinct from the static-cap `upstream_rq_retry_limit_exceeded`. **Byte-identical when no `retry_policy`** (a nil-guard — the 76-subtest differential is the regression anchor). The **AMEND-RT amendments** held as-built: (RT1) the enforced `retry_on` subset {5xx, gateway-error, connect-failure, reset, retriable-status-codes(+list)} distinguishes TRANSPORT failures from 5xx RESPONSES; parse-accept-defer {retriable-headers, envoy-ratelimited, grpc-*}; unknown tokens IGNORED (no PGV). (RT2) the +5 counters SCOPED + the 2 phase-41 stats flip LIVE. (RT4) the reference RANDOMIZES the RR initial offset ⇒ the EXHAUSTION arm is cross-side-EXACT (single 503 host: `upstream_rq_retry==N` + `limit_exceeded==1` + `total==N+1` + final 503), the RECOVER arm asserts the offset-invariant `downstream_rq_2xx==K`. (RT5) THIN reject surface (`retry_back_off.base_interval` required>0 + `max_interval<base_interval` boot-reject; `retry_on` freeform; NO new fuzzer). (RT7) `RX`/`URX` a RECORDED DEPARTURE (no response-flags plumbing — the phase-41 CB4 precedent; the differential asserts retry STATS + final status, never the access-log line). Differential **`0075-retry-loop`** (cross-side, TWO retry clusters: `c_exhaust` [single `HTTP503Responder` host] cross-side-EXACT + `c_recover` [2-host {503, echo} RR] offset-invariant `downstream_rq_2xx delta == recoverReqs` cross-side + subject-side `_retry_success == upstream_rq_retry > 0`); sleepless/count-based (the backoff is delay-only ⇒ count-immune); 2 `-count=1` deliberate breaks (A: `matches`→always-false; B: `IncUpstreamRqRetry` no-op) + 20/20 flake-free; NO new BackendKind (REUSE `HTTP503Responder` 35 + `HTTPEcho`). The §11 D-CB-* empirical pins were EXECUTED IN-SESSION (2026-06-19) live against `envoyproxy/envoy:contrib-v1.37.2` per `reference_docker_probe_bridge_network` (a slot-holding backend + concurrent load + `--mode validate` reject probes + a `/stats` name-roster scrape) + the go-control-plane v1.32.4 cache. **The headline finding NARROWED the enforced scope** (human-confirmed 2026-06-19): the BRAINSTORM Fork-1 "core overflow trio" (`max_connections` + `max_pending_requests` + `max_requests`) → the **`max_requests` keystone ONLY** — because the reference's fail-fast `503/UO` comes ONLY from a request budget (`max_connections` is a SOFT THROTTLE that PENDS rather than 503s; `max_pending_requests` needs the connection-pool QUEUE envoy-go's synchronous, queue-less model lacks), so cx/pending enforcement is DEFERRED to the future per-protocol connection-pooling family row (registered emit-0 for parity). **SPEC-firmed (AMEND-CB1..7):** (CB1) `max_requests`-only enforcement (the human-confirmed narrowing; cx/pending register-for-parity-but-defer). (CB2) a `max_requests` overflow increments **`upstream_rq_pending_overflow`** — the SAME counter as `max_pending_requests`; there is NO `upstream_rq_overflow` (the two request budgets differ only by gauge `rq_open` vs `rq_pending_open`). (CB3) stat surface **+14 → 1163** — the full parity block: 10 per-priority `*_open` gauges (`cx_open`/`cx_pool_open`/`rq_open`/`rq_pending_open`/`rq_retry_open` × {default, high}) + 4 cluster overflow counters (`upstream_cx_overflow`/`upstream_cx_pool_overflow`/`upstream_rq_pending_overflow`/`upstream_rq_retry_overflow`), scoped to `circuit_breakers` clusters (existing fixtures unaffected; a recorded departure — the reference emits the overflow counters on ALL clusters); only `default.rq_open` + `upstream_rq_pending_overflow` go LIVE, the other 12 emit 0 (the AMEND-OD3-4 emit-0-for-parity precedent); `remaining_*` gauges DEFERRED (`track_remaining`-gated). (CB4) overflow ⇒ `503` + `UO` flag + `upstream_reset_before_response_started{overflow}`; envoy-go emits the 503 via the existing local-reply path + sets `UO` where the `%RESPONSE_FLAGS%` accesslog surface allows (a PLAN/IMPL pin); the differential asserts the robust 503-status + stats pair, NOT the access-log line. (CB5) effective defaults 1024/1024/1024/3/∞ (via the `remaining_*` gauges) — `max_requests` defaults 1024 ⇒ the breaker is OFF unless explicitly small. (CB6) the PGV reject surface is THIN — only `Thresholds.priority` enum-range + `RetryBudget.budget_percent ∈ [0,100]` bite (budget `UInt32Value`s unbounded; duplicate-priority + `per_host_thresholds` accepted); envoy-go mirrors the 2 PGV rejects + adds a strict duplicate-priority reject + silent-ignores `per_host_thresholds`; NO new fuzzer (config-parse, not wire-decode). (CB7) +1 BackendKind `BlockingHoldResponder` (35 → 36 — holds a request until the driver releases) for the deterministic budget-fill. **Architecture (ADR-0248):** a per-priority `circuitBreaker` counter struct on `Cluster` (`cluster.go:85`); a synchronous `tryAcquireRequest(DEFAULT)` at router admission (`do{H1,H2}ClusterAction` — `router.go:588`/`router_h2.go:57`, before the upstream call) + a `defer releaseRequest` on completion (endpoint-free, NOT piggybacked on `RecordUpstreamResult`); fail-fast 503-UO + `upstream_rq_pending_overflow`++ + `rq_open`=1 on exhaustion; NO background goroutine; byte-identical when no `circuit_breakers`. The single ADR-0248 ABSORBS the BRAINSTORM's anticipated second ADR (ADR-0249 no longer needed at the narrowed scope). (Fork 2 — ARCHITECTURE → ADR-0248) a `circuitBreaker` struct on `Cluster` (`cluster.go:85`, alongside `health`/`outlier`) with per-priority atomic counters (`openConns`/`activeRequests`/`pendingRequests`) + the parsed budgets, NON-BLOCKING try-acquire/release at the existing lifecycle points (`max_requests` at router admission → release at `RecordUpstreamResult`; `max_connections` at `Dial` → release at conn-close; `max_pending_requests` in the admitted-but-no-connection window), fail-fast 503-UO + the matching `upstream_*_overflow` counter on exhaustion, the `*_open` gauge reads 1 at/over limit; NO background goroutine (synchronous atomic try-acquire — CONTRAST the phase-40.3 per-interval sweep goroutine) [parse in `buildCluster` `manager.go:363`, after `outlier_detection` `:416`]; (Fork 3 — PER-PRIORITY + STATS) ENFORCE DEFAULT-only (nothing routes by `RoutingPriority` yet), REGISTER BOTH `circuit_breakers.default.*` + `circuit_breakers.high.*` trees for surface parity (HIGH never binds, emits 0 — the AMEND-OD3-4 emit-0-for-parity precedent); As built: **ADR-0247** — the STATISTICAL passive-outlier detectors over the 40.1/40.2 substrate (ADR-0245/0246, REUSED UNCHANGED): the **success_rate detector** (`evalSuccessRate` — the eligible-host set [≥ `success_rate_minimum_hosts`, each ≥ `success_rate_request_volume`] + the cross-host mean + the **POPULATION stddev** + the `mean − (success_rate_stdev_factor/1000)×stddev` ejection threshold; a negative threshold is a no-op; ejects-by-default — `enforcing_success_rate` default 100) + the **failure_percentage detector** (`evalFailurePercentage` — the integer cross-multiplied `(total−success)*100 >= threshold*total` `>=` boundary over the eligible set; detect-only-by-default — `enforcing_failure_percentage` default 0). Both are **SWEEP-driven** (NOT per-request): the windowed per-host `(intervalTotal, intervalSuccess)` `atomic.Uint64` counters live on `hostHealth`, accumulated by a success/failure classification (success = NOT `localOriginErr` AND NOT 5xx) at the TOP of `record` on EVERY path; the per-interval `sweep()` snapshots-and-`Swap`-resets the windows, runs both evaluators in order, and `tryEject`s each flagged host CAS-once-only per sweep. The **per-interval aggregation runtime** is the project's FIRST outlier background goroutine: `Manager.StartOutlierDetection` (a sibling of `StartHealthChecks` with parallel `odCancel`/`odWG`, one `run(ctx)` goroutine per outlier cluster, no immediate t=0 sweep) + the `Drain` join + the `main.go:274` boot call (after `StartHealthChecks`); `-race`-clean, no leak. +8 stat registrations (1141 → **1149**): the 4 EXTERNAL driven `ejections_{detected,enforced}_{success_rate,failure_percentage}` + the 4 LOCAL-ORIGIN `ejections_{detected,enforced}_local_origin_{success_rate,failure_percentage}` (logic DEFERRED — registered unconditionally for surface parity, emit 0; AMEND-OD3-4) via the extended `registerStats` + `outlierStatNames()` (9 → 17). - **prior active-phase:** `phase 40 (outlier-detection) BRAINSTORM done` (2026-06-15) — the SECOND Upstream-robustness-family row: **passive** upstream health (`Cluster.outlier_detection`, `cluster.v3.OutlierDetection`, Cluster field 19) — eject hosts that fail on REAL traffic, the counterpart to phase-39 active health checks. REUSES the ADR-0242 `clusterHealth`/`hostHealth` registry + the ADR-0243 build-time-injected health-aware LB pick; ADDS one genuinely-new seam — a per-request upstream-outcome callback `cluster.RecordUpstreamResult(ep, UpstreamResult{StatusCode, LocalOriginErr})` from the H1/H2 router back to the cluster (the response status is observed in the router today but never threaded back). **Q-decisions:** (Q-subject) outlier detection (chosen over circuit breakers / retries+hedging / connection-pooling + the 3 now-unblocked health-gated LB candidates — it composes most directly with the phase-39 host health-state primitive: passive vs active health); (Q-scope) **full** outlier detection (all detector variants); (Q-split) **3 legs by detector class** — **40.1** = the passive seam + the ejection lifecycle + the single `consecutive_5xx` detector + the LB-filter extension (`isHealthy → available`) + `0069` [the minimal keystone, mirroring 39.1's single-HTTP-codec keystone]; **40.2** = the other consecutive detectors (`consecutive_gateway_failure` + `consecutive_local_origin_failure` + `split_external_local_origin_errors`); **40.3** = the statistical detectors (`success_rate` + `failure_percentage`) + a NEW per-interval cross-host mean/stddev aggregation runtime. **Architecture (Approach A → ADR-0245):** ejection is a SEPARATE per-host sub-state (new fields `ejected`/`unejectAtNanos`/`ejectCount`/`consec5xx` on `hostHealth`), DISTINCT from the active-HC `healthy` bit (outlier recovery is TIME-based — `base_ejection_time × num_ejections` linear backoff — not consecutive-success); `isHealthy`/`membership_healthy` KEEP their 39.1 active-HC-only meaning; the LB pick filters on `available = isHealthy && !isEjected` (the six constructs move their predicate `isHealthy → available`; byte-identical when no `outlier_detection`). The `UpstreamResult` seam struct is FORWARD-COMPATIBLE + fires on EVERY request so 40.2/40.3 don't re-touch the router (the 39.2 re-signature-churn lesson). **1 (possibly 2) ADRs anticipated:** ADR-0245 (the passive seam + the ejection dimension + the `available` LB-pick extension + the `consecutive_5xx` detector & ejection lifecycle); §Context at the 40.1 SPEC, body at the 40.1 IMPL per ADR-0044. Differential `0069-outlier-detection-consecutive-5xx` (a cluster {2 healthy backends, 1 always-5xx backend} + `outlier_detection{consecutive_5xx, base_ejection_time, max_ejection_percent}`; TRAFFIC-driven ejection — drive 5xx requests, POLL `/stats` until `ejections_active` converges on both sides [the `0066` poll-to-converge + warmup + delta-counter pattern, `reference_health_check_propagation_warmup`], then assert routing concentrates on the healthy hosts + cross-side `ejections_*` parity; 2 deliberate breaks; a time-based un-eject/recovery arm is a candidate-defer). **ROADMAP row 40 registers `in-progress`** (a flat family row WITH a pre-authorized 40.1/40.2/40.3 by-detector-class split; it flips `done` when all three legs land — NO parent rollup per ADR-0106). Anticipated moves at the 40.1 IMPL: fixtures 70 → 71 (`0069`), BackendKind tail 34 → 35 (an always-5xx responder — TBD at SPEC, D-OD-BACKEND), DECISIONS tail ADR-0244 → ADR-0245 (next-free ADR-0246), stat surface 1132 → ~1137 (~+4–6 `outlier_detection.*` — D-OD-STATS pins), fuzzers 42 → 42, ZERO new packages + ZERO new go.mod modules. The §10 D-OD-PROTO/LIFECYCLE/STATS/REJECT/BACKEND/SEAM pins to be EXECUTED at the 40.1 SPEC live against `contrib-v1.37.2` per `reference_docker_probe_bridge_network`. - **prior active-phase:** `phase 39.2 (health-check-tcp-grpc) IMPL done` (2026-06-15) — the 13-task TDD PLAN executed end-to-end subagent-driven, the six-gate GREEN, the full **70-dir differential GREEN**. As built: **ADR-0244** (the multi-codec `prober` dispatch — the TCP connect-only + gRPC `grpc.health.v1.Health/Check` checker codecs; the CODEC-ONLY widening of the 39.1 substrate). A small `prober` interface (`probe(addr) (ok, networkFailure bool)`) with three implementations: `httpProber` (the 39.1 `probeHTTP` body UNCHANGED), `tcpProber` (`net.Dialer{Timeout}.Dial("tcp", addr)` + immediate close — connect-only; connect-refused ⇒ `failure`+`network_failure` matching the HTTP dial-failure classification), `grpcProber` (a unary `grpc.health.v1.Health/Check` RPC over insecure H2; SERVING ⇒ ok; NOT_SERVING ⇒ `failure` [network_failure STAYS 0 — the application-vs-network discriminator]; `err != nil` ⇒ both++). `parseHealthChecks` returns a kind-tagged `[]checkerSpec{ envelope, prober }`; the runtime + transition model + the +7 stats + the panic threshold + the six-LB health view are BYTE-STABLE (the 39.1 substrate reused VERBATIM). The only new wiring: the gRPC cluster-must-be-H2 config-init reject (`<cluster> cluster must support HTTP/2 for gRPC healthchecking`) + the `send`/`receive` TCP payload DEPARTURE-reject; the 39.1 `only http_health_check is supported` reject LIFTED. Differential `0067-health-check-tcp` (connect-only TCP HC over {2 LIVE `HTTPEcho` backends [a bare TCP connect succeeds], 1 DEAD unbound port}; NO new BackendKind; poll-to-converge + warmup + delta-counter; 2 deliberate breaks; 20/20 flake-free) + `0068-health-check-grpc` (gRPC HC over an H2 cluster {2 LIVE `GRPCHealthResponder` backends [the +1 BackendKind — an h2c server answering `grpc.health.v1.Health/Check` ⇒ SERVING + returning 200 to plain-H2 data-plane requests], 1 DEAD unbound port}; poll-to-converge + warmup + per-side-exact `cx_active` pin [ref: 2 [pooled H2]; subj: 0 [idle teardown]] + delta-counter; 2 deliberate breaks; 20/20 flake-free). ADR-0244 §Decision/§Consequences landed IN-PLACE per ADR-0044. With 39.2 the active-HC checker roster (HTTP + TCP + gRPC) is COMPLETE. As-built counts: fixtures **68 → 70** (`0067`+`0068`); BackendKind tail **33 → 34** (`GRPCHealthResponder`); DECISIONS tail **ADR-0243 → ADR-0244** (next-free ADR-0245); stat surface **1132** (ZERO new — both codecs reuse the 39.1 codec-agnostic stat set); fuzzers **42** (UNCHANGED — the gRPC response is library-decoded, no hand-rolled wire parser); ZERO new packages + ZERO new go.mod modules. The Upstream-robustness family STAYS OPEN — 4 candidates remain after 39.2: {outlier detection, circuit breakers, retries + hedging, per-protocol connection pooling}. - **prior active-phase:** `phase 39 (upstream-health-check) BRAINSTORM done` (2026-06-14) — the FIRST Upstream-robustness-family row and the first NEW feature family opened since §9 (Network filters) closed at phase 33 (phases 34–38 were all Load-balancing-family rows). Charters `Cluster.health_checks` (`[]core.config.core.v3.HealthCheck`, proto field 8) — **active** upstream health checking (periodic outbound probes that mark hosts healthy/unhealthy) over an HTTP + TCP + gRPC checker roster — plus the project's FIRST per-host **health-state dimension** and the health-aware LB pick that makes all six LB constructs route only to healthy hosts (with a panic-threshold fallback). The family **keystone**: the host health-state primitive (i) unblocks the open Load-balancing family's 3 health-gated candidates {locality-weighted LB, priority load balancing, panic thresholds}, and (ii) is the substrate the sibling rows {outlier detection, circuit breakers, retries + hedging, connection pooling} compose with. **Q-decisions:** (Q0) NEW family = Upstream robustness (chosen over xDS/gRPC/HTTP3 — the foundational reliability layer that unblocks the health-gated LB candidates); (Q0b) first sub-project = active health checks (the keystone — establishes the health boundary; chosen over the cleaner-to-differential retries/circuit-breakers); (Q1) checker roster = HTTP + TCP + gRPC, split by codec — **39.1** = the keystone (host health-state dimension + the cluster-level checker runtime [the project's FIRST background runtime — `Manager.StartHealthChecks(ctx)` boot start + `Manager.Drain()` cancel/wait] + the HTTP probe codec + the health-aware LB across all six constructs) + the `0066-health-check-http` differential; **39.2** = the TCP + gRPC checkers reusing the 39.1 substrate; (Q2) health-aware LB = **Approach A: pick-time filter + panic fallback** (LB built once over all hosts; `Pick` skips unhealthy + re-picks; ring_hash/maglev walk-to-next-healthy preserving key stability; `healthy_panic_threshold` default 50% fallback-to-all), with the health view **build-time-injected** (host health is CLUSTER state, NOT a per-request `Pick` widening — the deliberate CONTRAST with the ADR-0235 hash-key / ADR-0239 subset-match ctx-carry pick-inputs; the `Pick` signature + exported `Cluster` surface stay BYTE-STABLE; behavior-neutral when all healthy → every existing fixture stays byte-identical). A NEW `health_checks` parse-reject surface (envoy-go's own byte-stable rejects; D-HC2). Differential `0066-health-check-http` (a cluster with a LIVE + a DEAD unbound host; POLL `/stats` until the dead host is unhealthy on BOTH sides [no fixed sleep], THEN assert 100% of traffic on the live host + cross-side `health_check.*`/`membership_*` parity; 3 `-count=1` deliberate breaks; ≥20-run flake-free). **2 ADRs anticipated:** ADR-0242 (the host health-state dimension + the active-HC runtime) + ADR-0243 (the health-aware LB pick — Approach A); the phase-36/38 two-ADR shape; §Context at SPEC, bodies at IMPL per ADR-0044. Anticipated moves at the 39.1 IMPL: fixtures 67 → 68 (`0066`), DECISIONS tail ADR-0241 → ADR-0243 (next-free ADR-0244), stat surface 1125 → ~1131 (~+6 `health_check.*` + `membership_healthy`; D-HC4 pins), fuzzers 42 → 42, BackendKind tail 33 → 33; ZERO new packages + ZERO new go.mod modules at 39.1. **ROADMAP row 39 registers `in-progress`** (a flat family row WITH a pre-authorized 39.1/39.2 by-codec split; it flips to `done` per-leg at each IMPL six-gate — NO parent rollup per ADR-0106). - **prior active-phase:** `phase 38.2 (weighted-clusters) IMPL done` (2026-06-14) — the 10-task TDD PLAN executed end-to-end subagent-driven, the six-gate GREEN. As built: **ADR-0241** — the **weighted-cluster routing primitive** (`RouteAction.weighted_clusters`) + the `ClusterWeight.metadata_match` merge composition. The project's **FIRST per-request cluster-SELECTION primitive** (the SECOND `RouteAction.ClusterSpecifier` arm; every prior route resolves a SINGLE cluster at config-build). A route fans across N weighted clusters, ONE chosen per request by integer weight — weighted-RANDOM via a per-action `weightedSelector` (cumulative weights + a `newPCGRNG()` draw in `[0, Σweight)` + a cumulative walk — the FIRST use of the `randomLB` per-request RNG model OUTSIDE the cluster LB). `total_weight` is DEPRECATED upstream and IGNORED (the client uses the SUM). Each `ClusterWeight.metadata_match` MERGES over the route-level `RouteAction.metadata_match` (entry precedence; merged ONCE at config-build per entry; rides the EXISTING ADR-0239 `cluster.WithSubsetMatch` ctx seam UNCHANGED — NO `loadBalancer.Pick` change, NO exported-cluster change — the 38.1 compose). New types: `weightedSelector` + `H1/H2WeightedClusterAction` in `internal/filter/http/router/router_weighted.go` + `weightedClusterRouteAction` bridge in `internal/filter/hcm/actions.go` + `buildWeightedRouterAction`/`mergeRouteSubsetMatch`/`buildWeightedBridge` in `internal/filter/hcm/config.go`. SIX producer parse-reject arms (empty clusters / name-required / cluster_header-unsupported / weight-required / sum-0 / dangling — all unit-level; NO boot-reject fixture). Differential `0065-weighted-clusters` (per-side weight-distribution band over 3 clusters at ~4.5σ margins + a metadata_match-composition arm + cross-side conservation; 3 `-count=1` deliberate breaks [swap-weights / drop-cluster / drop-compose]; 20/20 flake-free). ZERO new stat surface (surface 1125 → 1125 — the FIRST LB-family leg to add NO stat surface). The ADR-0241 §Decision/§Consequences bodies landed IN-PLACE per ADR-0044. As-built counts: fixtures **66 → 67** (`0065`); DECISIONS tail **ADR-0240 → ADR-0241** (next-free ADR-0242); stat surface 1125 → **1125** (ZERO new); fuzzers 42 (UNCHANGED); BackendKind tail 33 (UNCHANGED). The **4 `lb_subsets_*` stats** (`active` gauge + `created`/`selected`/`fallback` counters; `active`/`created` Set/Add at build to the ×1 distinct-subset count — NOT the reference's 33× artifact; `selected`/`fallback` the FIRST stats a `loadBalancer` Inc's from its own `Pick` path, request-counted + cross-side-equal). ZERO new cluster reject arm (CLUSTER_PROVIDED pre-covered; the only new reject is the producer-side `router-metadata-match-nonscalar`). The phase-38 SPEC (prior commit `f52a8ea`) executed the §11 D-SS1..D-SS7 empirical pins live + anchored the ADR-0239/0240 §Context drafts + CONSUMED the FINAL 38.1/38.2 split. **SPEC-firmed deltas vs the BRAINSTORM:** the parse-reject surface is **EMPTY** — the only reference reject (`lb_subset_config` + `lb_policy: CLUSTER_PROVIDED` → `cluster: LB policy CLUSTER_PROVIDED cannot be combined with lb_subset_config`) is ALREADY covered by envoy-go's pre-existing unsupported-policy gate, so ZERO new reject arm and the `Cluster.LbPolicy` supported-list + `TestManager_Error_UnsupportedLBPolicy` are UNTOUCHED (PGV imposes nothing beyond 3 enum-range checks; empty keys / empty selectors / duplicate selectors / fallback-default mismatches ALL accept); `envoy.lb` value-equality is double-valued for numbers (int 1 ≡ 1.0 — no canonicalization) with an exact key-set match; stat surface anticipated **1121 → 1125** (+4 `lb_subsets_*`: `active` gauge + `created`/`selected`/`fallback` counters — the reference's `active`/`created` carry a **33× magnitude artifact** envoy-go does NOT mirror, pinning the ×1 distinct-subset count; only `selected`/`fallback` [request-counted] cross-side-equal in `0064`); the `lb_subsets_selected`/`fallback` counters are the FIRST stats a `loadBalancer` Inc's from its own `Pick` path (injected at register; ADR-0024 noted, not amended). **ROADMAP row 38 STAYS `in-progress`** (it flips at the 38.1 IMPL six-gate — a flat family row, NO parent rollup per ADR-0106). Charters the project's **FIFTH LB construct** and the family's **FIRST wrapper**: `Cluster.lb_subset_config` (`envoy.config.cluster.v3`) — **NOT a `Cluster.LbPolicy` enum value** (unlike the five incumbents roundRobin/leastRequest/randomLB/ringHashLB/maglevLB) but a wrapper triggered by the presence of `lb_subset_config`, that selects a subset of endpoints matching the route's `metadata_match` and **delegates to the cluster's `lb_policy` as the child policy run within that subset**. Picked from the 4-candidate roster (Q0) as **the only candidate decoupled from the absent health-checking boundary** (the other three — locality-weighted / priority / panic — are health-gated and belong after the Upstream-robustness family). As chartered: a `subsetLB` wrapper (`internal/cluster/subset.go` — enumerate subsets per selector × distinct endpoint-metadata value-tuples; a child `loadBalancer` per subset built by the existing policy factory — **ALL FIVE child policies supported** [Q1=A], the hashKey passing through to a hash-policy child; resolve the route match → subset → delegate `child.Pick`; the cluster-level fallback {NO_FALLBACK/ANY_ENDPOINT/DEFAULT_SUBSET} + `default_subset`) over a NEW `Endpoint.Metadata` dimension (the `envoy.lb` namespace captured at `extractEndpoints`, `manager.go:533` — the FIRST per-endpoint dimension beyond `Host:Port`) + a SECOND LB pick-seam input (the resolved subset match widening `Pick(hashKey, hasHash)` → `Pick(hashKey, hasHash, match, hasMatch)` + `WithSubsetMatch`/`subsetMatchFrom`, the ADR-0235 hash-key STYLE TEMPLATE) + a NEW HTTP producer plane (`RouteAction.metadata_match["envoy.lb"]` attached to `clusterRouteAction` — `actions.go:201`). The manager wraps-after-switch (subset wraps the `lb_policy` child — NOT a new `case`). **NO lb_policy reject-text change** (subset is not an enum value — the phase-34/35/36/37 doubly-hit reject-text-retarget lineage BREAKS; the reject surface shifts to `lb_subset_config` validation, D-SS1). **Pre-authorized 38.1/38.2 by-plane split (Q2/Q2b — the phase-36 valve):** 38.1 = subset LB on the HTTP `RouteAction.metadata_match` plane (this leg); 38.2 = `RouteAction.weighted_clusters` routing + `WeightedCluster.ClusterWeight.metadata_match` (a NEW per-request weighted-cluster-selection primitive — the router resolves a SINGLE cluster per route today; the brainstorm surfaced this hidden prerequisite). Differential `0064-lb-subset` (HTTP routes each carrying a `metadata_match` pinned to a subset over `envoy.lb`-tagged endpoints): subset affinity (every served backend ∈ the expected subset, BOTH sides — the SET-membership property; the `metadata_match` is STATIC config → NAT-transparent → TRUE cross-side) + a fallback arm + cross-side byte-equiv + a cross-side `StatsAsserter` (the `lb_subsets_*` stats). 2 ADRs anticipated: **ADR-0239** (the subset-match seam-input extension + the `RouteAction.metadata_match` producer — the seam-BUILD half, parallel to ADR-0235) + **ADR-0240** (the subset LB policy + the `Endpoint.Metadata` dimension — the policy half, parallel to ADR-0236/0238); the phase-36 two-ADR shape (contrast phases 35/37's single-ADR reuse); §Context at SPEC, bodies at IMPL per ADR-0044. Anticipated moves at the 38.1 IMPL: stat surface **1121 → ~1125** (~+4 `lb_subsets_*` stats — D-SS4 pins), fixtures **65 → 66** (`0064`; NO boot-reject dir anticipated — the parse rejects land unit-level), fuzzers UNCHANGED **42** (no wire decode — a subset-resolution property test is unit-level), BackendKind tail UNCHANGED **33** (`0064` reuses the `0063` HTTP backends), ZERO new packages + ZERO new go.mod deps. **The Load-balancing family stays OPEN** — **3** candidates remain after 38 {locality-weighted LB, priority load balancing, panic thresholds} (all health-gated).
